### [39] [Tone-Conditioned Curriculum Learning for Low-Resource Bantu Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31642)
*Kesego Mokgosi,Vukosi Marivate,Sitwala Mundia,Unarine Netshifhefhe,Tsholofelo Hope Mogale,Thapelo Sindane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对南部班图语系6种语言（使用者超8000万）的自动语音识别问题，提出音调条件课程框架，结合混合难度评分、门控适配器和分阶段课程训练。实验表明W2V-BERT在恩古尼语系表现更优，Whisper在索托-茨瓦纳语系更佳，最佳平均WER达28.41%。研究建议根据语言特性选择模型并进行跨语料库验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南部班图语系使用者超8000万，但现有基础ASR模型的零样本WER超过100%，严重限制了教育和公共服务中的应用，需要专门针对该语系的语音识别技术突破。

Method: 提出音调条件课程框架，针对6种南部班图语系语言，采用混合难度评分、门控适配器驱动、基于音调统计的分阶段课程训练策略，在社区语料库上训练并迁移测试到NCHLT语料库以评估跨语料库鲁棒性。

Result: W2V-BERT在恩古尼语系比Whisper优3-4个WER点，而Whisper在索托-茨瓦纳语系表现更好；音调条件增强的W2V-BERT在所有数据集上达到28.41%平均WER，在希特颂加语迁移测试中达23.79%。

Conclusion: 没有单一模型能适用于所有6种语言，部署时需根据语言特性选择模型并进行跨语料库验证。架构与语言之间存在明显交互效应，音调信息对提升识别性能具有重要作用。

Abstract: Southern Bantu languages are spoken by over 80 million people, yet current foundation ASR models still produce zero-shot WER above 100%, which limits practical use in education and public services. We addressed this gap with a tone conditioned curriculum framework for 6 Southern Bantu languages that combined hybrid difficulty scoring, gated adapters driven by tonal statistics and staged curriculum training. We trained on a community corpus and tested transfer to NCHLT to measure robustness beyond matched evaluation. Results revealed clear interactions between architecture and language, with W2V-BERT outperforming Whisper on Nguni languages by 3 to 4 WER points whilst Whisper performed better on Sotho-Tswana languages. W2V-BERT with tone conditioning reached 28.41% average WER across datasets and 23.79% on Xitsonga transfer. No single model suited all 6 languages, so deployment should pair model selection per language with validation across corpora.

</details>


### [40] [Moral Safety in LLMs: Exposing Performative Compliance with Puzzled Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31644)
*Mohammadamin Shafiei,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了大语言模型在道德评估中存在的"表演性服从"现象：当明确标注人口统计身份时模型表现公平，但需要推断身份时公平性显著下降（有害决策增加4.4个百分点），这表明当前公平性评估高估了模型的道德安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在医疗、法律、招聘等高风险场景中的应用日益广泛，必须准确评估其道德行为是否真实可靠，而非表面合规。

Method: 提出"线索变异方法"（cue-variation methodology），固定道德困境和人口统计身份，仅改变身份信息的表达方式（显式标签vs推断），并引入"线索可见性差距"（Cue Visibility Gap）作为模型无关的鲁棒性指标。

Result: 隐藏显式标签后，有害决策增加4.4个百分点，模型安全排名发生改变，且即使模型正确推断出人口统计身份，这种转变仍然存在。

Conclusion: 当前公平性评估仅测量表面合规性而非道德鲁棒性，不应作为高风险场景部署决策的依据；需要将线索变异纳入公平性基准测试。

Abstract: As large language models take on morally consequential roles in healthcare, legal, and hiring contexts, we need to examine whether their ethical behaviors are genuine or superficial. We show that current fairness evaluations substantially overestimate moral safety. Models appear fair when demographic identity is stated as an explicit label, yet become measurably less fair when the same identity must be inferred. We term this failure \emph{performative compliance}, where a model is fair when the presentation resembles a fairness evaluation and less fair as that cue weakens. We introduce a cue-variation methodology that holds the moral dilemma and the demographic identity fixed and varies only how that identity is conveyed. Hiding the explicit label raises harmful decisions by $+4.4$~pp and changes model safety rankings, and the shift persists when models correctly infer the demographic, ruling out attribution error. We propose the \textbf{Cue Visibility Gap}, a model-agnostic robustness metric that can be added to any existing fairness benchmark to separate genuine from performative moral safety. Fairness evaluations that omit cue variation measure surface compliance, not moral robustness, and should not ground deployment decisions in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [41] [Overview of the TalentCLEF 2026: Skill and Job Title Intelligence for Human Capital Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31692)
*Luis Gasco,Hermenegildo Fabregat,Laura García-Sardiña,Paula Estrella,Warre Veys,Casimiro Pío Carrino,Matthias De Lange,Daniel Deniz Cerpa,Álvaro Rodrigo,Jens-Joris Decorte,Rabih Zbib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TalentCLEF 2026第二届竞赛，包含职位-候选人匹配和职位-技能匹配两个任务，吸引113个团队和400+提交，旨在推进人力资源管理领域的NLP研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进人力资源管理（HCM）领域的自然语言处理研究，为该领域提供共享评估基准

Method: 设计两个任务：(A) 情境化职位-候选人匹配，用英文和西班牙文从简历中识别和排序最合适候选人；(B) 职位-技能匹配并进行技能类型分类，用英文区分核心技能和情境技能

Result: 吸引了113个注册团队，两个任务共收到超过400份提交，表明研究社区对HCM领域共享评估基准的兴趣日益增长

Conclusion: 本文描述了竞赛动机和组织、数据集与评估设置，并报告了参赛团队的主要成果，为HCM领域的NLP评估提供了基准参考

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the second edition of the TalentCLEF challenge, organized as a Lab at the Conference and Labs of the Evaluation Forum (CLEF) 2026. TalentCLEF is an initiative aimed at advancing Natural Language Processing research in Human Capital Management. The second edition of the challenge consisted of two tasks: Task A, contextualized job-person matching, focuses on identifying and ranking the most suitable candidates represented by their resumes for a given job vacancy in English and Spanish. Task B, job-skill matching with skill type classification, addresses retrieving the most relevant skills for a given job title in English and distinguishing between core and contextual skills. TalentCLEF attracted 113 registered teams and received more than 400 submissions in the two tasks, reflecting the growing interest of the research community in shared evaluation benchmarks for Human Capital Management. This paper describes the motivation and organization of the challenge, summarizes the datasets and evaluation settings, and reports the main results obtained by the participating teams.

</details>


### [42] [Cross-lingual Relation Extraction with Large Language Models: Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, and Fine-Tuned Evaluation on Romanian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31718)
*Dragos-Mitrut Vasile,Elena-Simona Apostol,Stefan-Adrian Toma,Adrian Paschke,Ciprian-Octavian Truica*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究跨语言关系抽取在低资源语言（罗马尼亚语）上的可行性，通过数据集翻译和LLM推理结合，评估Gemma 4 31B和多种编码器模型。结果表明QLoRA微调可提升F1超过22个百分点，小型编码器模型（125M-560M参数）性能接近大型模型，31B模型在部署场景中性价比不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如罗马尼亚语）缺乏标注语料库，制约了关系抽取任务的发展。需要探索跨语言RE的可行性，以减轻对目标语言标注数据的依赖。

Method: 1) 使用LLM翻译流水线将SemEval-2010 Task 8数据集从英语翻译为罗马尼亚语；2) 评估Gemma 4 31B模型在zero-shot、few-shot和QLoRA微调三种配置下的表现；3) 对比四种编码器基线模型（XLM-RoBERTa-base/large、Romanian BERT、RoBERTa-large，125M-560M参数）；4) 评估两种任务形式：标记实体的关系分类和端到端抽取。

Result: 1) 罗马尼亚语在prompt-only设置下相比英语下降3-5个百分点；2) few-shot prompting相比zero-shot提升有限；3) QLoRA微调使两种语言的macro F1提升超过22个百分点，同时将跨语言差距从3.3pp缩小至1.4pp；4) 编码器基线模型在罗马尼亚语上与QLoRA Gemma差距仅1-4pp，但参数量小50-250倍；5) 125M参数的罗马尼亚语BERT与278M参数的XLM-R性能相当。

Conclusion: 在计算资源受限的部署场景下，使用31B参数模型进行单任务关系抽取缺乏充分理由。小型专用编码器模型是更具性价比的选择，尤其是针对单一语言的场景。

Abstract: Relation extraction (RE) for low-resource languages is typically constrained by the lack of annotated corpora. We investigate the feasibility of cross-lingual RE for Romanian by combining automatic dataset translation with large language model (LLM) inference. We translate the SemEval-2010 Task 8 benchmark from English to Romanian using an LLM-based translation pipeline and evaluate Gemma 4 31B under zero-shot, few-shot, and QLoRA fine-tuned configurations, against four encoder baselines spanning 125M to 560M parameters: XLM- RoBERTa (base and large), Romanian BERT, and RoBERT- large. We assess two task formulations: relation classification with marked entities and end-to-end extraction. Our results show that Romanian incurs a 3 to 5 percentage point (pp) drop relative to English in prompt-only settings, that few-shot prompting provides marginal gains over zero-shot, and that QLoRA fine-tuning improves macro F1-Score by more than 22 percentage points in both languages while reducing the cross-lingual gap from 3.3 to 1.4pp. The encoder baselines come within 1-4pp of QLoRA Gemma on Romanian despite being 50-250 times smaller, with monolingual Romanian BERT at 125M parameters matching multilingual XLM-R at 278M. The case for using a 31B model for single-task RE on Romanian is therefore weak in deployment scenarios where compute matters. We release the translated dataset, evaluation code, and trained models.

</details>


### [43] [Seeing Is Not Sharing: Some Vision-Language Models Overestimate Common Ground in Asymmetric Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31719)
*Nan Li,Albert Gatt,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨视觉-语言模型(VLMs)在协作对话中通过grounding区分"可能共享"与"已共享"信息的能力。研究发现，提供地图图像或文本描述都会使模型过度预测一致性，将潜在共同ground与已建立的共同ground混淆。模型依赖静态指代线索而非追踪对话历史中的grounding过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作对话中，共享感知不等于共享理解，必须通过交互建立相互理解。当前VLMs是否能区分"可能共享"与"已共享"信息这一基本问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 将问题形式化为解释匹配任务，使用13,077个来自HCRC MapTask对话的标注引用表达。通过系统控制对话语境和地图信息访问条件，评估多个VLMs（包括Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct等5个模型），分别测试真实地图图像、文本描述和无信息图像三种条件。

Result: 地图图像或文本描述均改善整体性能但导致过度预测对齐；无信息图像完全抑制对齐预测，说明偏差由任务相关地图内容驱动而非视觉通道。性能提升以牺牲非对齐案例准确性为代价。校准分析和引用链追踪表明模型依赖静态指代线索而非追踪对话历史中的grounding演变。

Conclusion: 当模型展示此偏差时，地图内容（无论以视觉还是文本形式呈现）被当作共同理解的证据，将"潜在"ground与"已建立"ground混为一谈。这表明当前VLMs在协作对话grounding方面存在根本性局限。

Abstract: In collaborative dialogue, shared perception does not guarantee shared interpretation. Mutual understanding must be established through interaction. We investigate whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish what could be shared from what has been shared between dialogue participants through grounding. We formulate this as an interpretation-matching task on 13,077 annotated reference expressions from HCRC MapTask dialogues, and evaluate VLMs under systematically controlled manipulations of dialogue context and map-information access. Our results show that providing authentic map images improves overall performance but shifts models toward over-predicting alignment. Textual descriptions of the same map content reproduce this bias, while non-informative images suppress alignment predictions entirely, indicating that the bias is driven by task-relevant map content, not the visual channel. This improvement comes at the cost of degraded accuracy on non-aligned cases. Calibration analysis and reference-chain tracking further suggest that models rely on static referential cues on the maps rather than tracking how grounding unfolds through dialogue history. We observe these patterns most clearly in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and, to varying degrees, in four additional models from two architecture families. In models that exhibit the bias, map content, whether presented visually or textually, is treated as evidence of mutual understanding, conflating potential with established common ground.

</details>


### [44] [Adapting Foundation ASR Models to Dysarthric Speech: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31722)
*Christian Huber,Laura Kernahan,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对运动障碍性构音障碍(Dysarthric)语音的自动语音识别(ASR)问题，通过对Whisper基础模型进行个性化微调，显著提升了识别性能，最佳Word Error Rate达到9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动障碍性构音障碍(Dysarthric)语音的自动语音识别(ASR)系统性能较差，严重限制了受影响说话者在日常交流中的使用便利性。

Method: 使用TEQST工具收集了一位构音障碍说话者92小时的朗读语音数据，随后通过移动应用收集了8.8小时的用户纠正数据。采用Whisper作为基础模型进行微调，同时对比了LoRA适应和Qwen3-ASR作为基础模型的效果。

Result: 仅用1.4小时适应数据即可将Word Error Rate降至15.8%；使用22.5小时数据进一步降至10.7%；使用全部数据（含纠正数据）达到最佳结果9.7%。LoRA适应和Qwen3-ASR在该场景下表现均较差。

Conclusion: 个性化微调可使基础ASR模型显著提升对构音障碍语音的识别效果，适合实际部署应用。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often perform poorly in dysarthric speech, limiting their usefulness to affected speakers in everyday communication. This paper presents a personalized ASR system for a dysarthric speaker, built by adapting a foundation ASR model to speaker-specific data. Using the TEQST tool, we collected 92 hours of read speech and later added 8.8 hours of user corrections gathered through a deployed mobile application. Starting from Whisper, fine-tuning reduced word error rate to 15.8% with only 1.4 hours of adaptation data, reached 10.7% with 22.5 hours, and achieved the best result of 9.7% when using all available data including the corrections. Using LoRA adaptation and/or Qwen3-ASR as foundation model performed worse in this setting. The results show that personalized fine-tuning can make foundation ASR models substantially more effective for dysarthric speech and suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [45] [STEB: Style Text Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31741)
*Rafael Rivera Soto,Anna Wegmann,Cristina Aggazzotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STEB是一个用于标准化风格嵌入评估的开源基准测试,包含96个数据集、7种语言,发现语义嵌入在风格任务中表现不佳,且没有单一的Style Embedding在所有任务中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义嵌入在MTEB上有严格评估,但风格嵌入的评估碎片化,每项工作都依赖自己的任务和数据集,缺乏统一的评估标准。

Method: 提出Style Text Embedding Benchmark(STEB),包含96个跨7种语言的数据集,涵盖作者身份验证、作者身份检索、AI文本检测、语言特征探测等应用场景,并开源代码。

Result: 语义嵌入在风格任务中普遍表现不佳;没有一种风格嵌入在所有评估任务中始终最优。

Conclusion: 风格嵌入评估需要标准化;该工作为风格嵌入研究提供了统一的评估框架和开源工具。

Abstract: While semantic embeddings are rigorously evaluated on the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, the evaluation of style embeddings remains fragmented, with each work relying on their own set of tasks and datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Style Text Embedding Benchmark, a comprehensive open-source benchmark intended to standardize the evaluation of style embeddings. STEB encompasses 96 datasets across 7 languages, spanning applications such as authorship verification, authorship retrieval, AI-text detection, probing of linguistic features, and others. We find that semantic embeddings consistently fail in stylistic tasks, and that there is no style embedding that is universally superior across all tasks evaluated. We open-source the STEB code base at: https://github.com/rrivera1849/STEB.

</details>


### [46] [CHERRY: Compressed Hierarchical Experts with Recurrent Representational Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31796)
*Dohyeon Kwon,Youngjin Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三种训练计算高效语言模型的技术：(1)选择性监督通过聚焦15%语义负载token实现4.5x效率提升；(2)深度压缩将48层1B模型压缩至6层227M并通过循环恢复保持性能；(3)混合高效专家(MoEE)融合多个压缩模型进一步提升。实验在韩语基础模型CHERRY-1.8B上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练需要大量计算资源，如何在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本和参数量是关键挑战。现有方法在效率与性能间存在权衡。

Method: 三种互补技术：(1)选择性真值token训练(SGT)，仅对15%语义负载token施加监督，通过梯度耦合使其余85% token也获得提升；(2)深度压缩，将48层1B参数模型压缩至6层227M，通过学习的循环展开恢复性能；(3)混合高效专家(MoEE)，将多个压缩模型组装成专家混合系统进行多token预测。

Result: (1) 15%监督token可恢复约67%全序列loss降低，效率提升4.5x；(2) 6层压缩模型(34个有效循环层)达到held-out loss 2.934，接近566M密集模型的2.926；(3) 2专家MoEE达到loss 2.789，优于最佳单一压缩模型。

Conclusion: 三种技术均在韩语模型上验证有效，可显著提升训练计算效率。方法适用于特定场景(韩语、loss指标)，scope限于单一模型家族。

Abstract: We study three complementary techniques for training compute-efficient language models.
  (1) Selective supervision and per-token efficiency. Selective Ground Truth Token Training (SGT) concentrates supervision on the ~15% of output tokens that carry semantic payload. Through positive gradient coupling in position-shared transformer weights -- a token-level instance of auxiliary-task transfer -- the remaining 85% of unsupervised tokens still improve substantially, giving a 4.5x per-supervised-token efficiency (at the step-100 eval optimum, ~67% of the full-sequence loss reduction is recovered from 15% of the supervision). We prove that this improvement on unsupervised tokens is guaranteed whenever the gradient coupling coefficient gamma-bar = 0.72 is positive (Theorem 1), and show the effect is a property of natural-language structure: it collapses on shuffled text.
  (2) Depth compression with recurrent recovery. A 48-layer, 1B-parameter transformer is compressed to 6 layers (227M) by averaging adjacent layers and restored through learned recurrent unrolling. With 34 effective recurrent layers it reaches a held-out loss of 2.934, within measurement noise of a 566M dense model at 2.926 -- a 2.5x reduction in parameters.
  (3) Fusion of compressed experts. Assembling several compressed models as a Mixture of Efficient Experts (MoEE) with multi-token prediction improves over each single expert at comparable active parameters: a 2-expert MoEE reaches loss 2.789 versus 2.926 for the best single compressed model.
  We validate these techniques on CHERRY-1.8B, a Korean foundation model whose every trainable parameter derives from our own training runs. We are explicit throughout about the scope of the evidence (one model family, Korean data, loss-based metrics) and about which claims are established versus prospective.

</details>


### [47] [Explicit Fuzzy Logic in the Feed-Forward Layer: Self-Forgetting Quantifiers Discover Legible Grammatical-Licensing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31845)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用带有一阶逻辑操作（集合交集和差集）的否定能力前馈网络(NC-FFN)替代Transformer的FFN层，并加入序列量词实现语法许可检测，在保持性能的同时大幅提升模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的前馈(FFN)子层虽然聚合了注意力机制的输出，但缺乏对计算内容的可解释性说明。作者认为需要一种参数中性、逻辑透明的机制来解释FFN的工作原理。

Method: 1.用模糊集合操作替换传统FFN：隐藏单元执行交集A*B和差集A*(1-B)操作，后者提供有界否定（"A但非B"）。2.添加序列量词模块：软存在量词和软比例量词，每个单元有可学习的遗忘率（从粘性初始化）。3.通过参数中性设计保持模型容量不变。

Result: 1.在N位奇偶校验任务上达到最参数高效的推理基础。2.在大规模（125M参数，OpenWebText）上NC-FFN与GELU基线达到相同的困惑度。3.解决了两大限制：语法缺失（许可和量词相关）恢复至epoch 1水平（将epoch 2差距减半），在LAMBADA上略有提升。4.语义层单元无需字典学习即可解释为语法许可检测器：每个单元在触发词（比较级、被动分词、否定极性词）上激活并携带记忆预测许可词。

Conclusion: 论文成功构建了参数中性且保持语言模型质量的Transformer，具有可读、可解释的语法机制。FFN结构现可被解读：量词衰减遵循粘性初始化（半衰期约1.5 tokens），逻辑操作定位于第0层。全布尔FFN会导致训练发散，但通过此设计在保持性能的同时实现了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: A transformer's feed-forward (FFN) sublayer materializes the distinctions attention gathers, yet gives no account of what it computes. In a parameter-neutral replacement, each hidden unit is an explicit fuzzy set operation on sigmoid-bounded [0,1] memberships: intersection A*B and set-difference A*(1-B), the latter a bounded positive negation ("A but not B") that gated/bilinear units lack -- a negation-capable FFN (NC-FFN). On N-bit parity they are the most parameter-efficient reasoning basis at shallow depth; at scale (125M, OpenWebText) NC-FFN ties the GELU baseline's perplexity, every unit carrying explicit logical form. Two limits share one cause: two-operand logic localizes to layer 0 and erodes under training, and the one robust grammatical deficit concentrates in licensing and quantifiers, beyond within-token operators. We resolve both with a small block of sequence quantifiers: a soft existential and a soft proportion, each with a per-unit learned forgetting rate from a sticky init. This recovers the deficit at epoch one (halving the wider epoch-two gap), modestly leads on LAMBADA, and makes the FFN legible: the structure now holds and migrates into depth; the decay un-learns its stickiness (median half-life ~1.5 tokens; zero latch units); and at the semantic layers the units read, without dictionary learning, as grammatical licensing detectors: each fires on a licensor (a comparative, a passive participle, a negative-polarity item) and carries its memory forward to predict the licensed word (than, by, nor). This legibility is localized and free only up to a partition (a fully Boolean FFN diverges in training), but the result is a parameter-neutral, language-model-quality transformer with a readable, interpretable-by-construction grammatical mechanism -- an account not just of what a feed-forward layer represents but how it licenses.

</details>


### [48] [Theory of Mind and Persuasion Beyond Conversation: Assessing the Capacity of LLMs to Induce Belief States via Planning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31916)
*Ben Slater,Matteo G. Mecattaf,Lucy G. Cheke,John Burden,Winnie Street*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NCP-ToM（非对话式规划心智理论）评估框架，测试LLM智能体通过行动而非对话诱导目标信念的能力。六种前沿模型和人类参与者在600个任务中进行测试，GPT-5表现最佳（约80%准确率），是唯一超越人类水平的模型，但鲁棒性仍不如人类。所有模型在真信念任务上表现优于假信念任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ToM基准测试采用被动问答格式，无法评估LLM作为自主智能体时的社会推理能力。随着LLM被部署为更具代理性的自主系统，需要新的评估范式来测试智能体通过行动诱导他人信念的能力，这对用户助手交互、教育场景等应用至关重要，但也可能带来操控或虚假信息风险。

Method: 使用NCP-ExploreToM框架，将传统任务结构反转：提供一组信念状态目标，要求模型通过移动物体或引导角色进入房间来达成目标。评估了6种前沿模型（包括GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude 4系列）和人类参与者的表现，覆盖600个任务实例。

Result: GPT-5在智能体设置中约80%任务成功，是唯一超越人类参与者的模型，但在跨情境鲁棒性上仍不如人类。所有模型（包括人类）在真信念任务上表现优于假信念任务。

Conclusion: LLM在非对话式任务完成方面展现出新兴的社会推理能力。智能体评估对于理解自主社交智能体的安全性和对齐至关重要，NCP-ToM范式可有效补充现有ToM评估，并为对齐研究提供积极信号（模型在真信念任务上的优势）。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on passive question-answering formats, but the deployment of LLMs in increasingly agentic and autonomous forms demands new evaluations. In this paper we evaluate an agent's ability to induce specific belief states in other agents by taking actions rather than using conversational persuasion, a capability we call Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). NCP-ToM is likely to be essential for many agent use-cases, including within user-assistant interactions and pedagogical contexts, but may also present manipulation or misinformation risks. Using a novel framework, NCP-ExploreToM, we subvert the conventional task structure by providing models with a set of belief state goals and requiring them to move objects or direct characters into rooms to achieve their goals. We evaluated six frontier models, including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro and the Claude 4 series, and a cohort of human participants, across 600 task instances. GPT-5 was successful on approximately 80% of tasks in the agentic setting, and was the only model to outperform human participants on our task, but was still less robust than humans across contexts. We additionally found that all models, like humans, performed better on tasks inducing true belief states than false belief states, which is a positive signal for alignment efforts. These findings highlight emerging social-reasoning capabilities in LLMs for non-conversational task completion and underscore the necessity of agentic evaluations for understanding the safety and alignment of autonomous social agents.

</details>


### [49] [LuxEmo: Expressive Text-to-Speech Corpus for Luxembourgish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31947)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Sandipana Dowerah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LuxEmo，一个用于卢森堡语的21小时对话式情感语音语料库，包含4种情感类别。该语料库来源于RTL青年广播节目，采用半自动流程构建，包括语音活动检测、去噪、语言识别、ASR分割、情感预测等步骤，并针对5种 expressive TTS系统进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流语音数据集主要关注广泛使用的语言，低资源语言如卢森堡语在语音技术研究中严重不足，缺乏专门的情感语音数据，限制了相关语音合成技术的发展。

Method: 使用半自动策划工作流程，结合语音活动检测、去噪、语言识别、基于LuxASR的分段、自动情感预测、词汇线索和针对性人工审核。语料库来源于Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL)青年广播节目，采用自动检测加人工验证的方式。

Result: 成功构建了21小时卢森堡语对话式情感语音语料库，包含4种情感类别。基准测试覆盖了5种expressive TTS系统，包括基于德语的跨语言迁移、多语言卢森堡语支持、卢森堡语适配和非参数韵律迁移等方案。

Conclusion: LuxEmo填补了卢森堡语情感语音数据的空白，为低资源语言的expressive speech研究提供了重要资源，benchmark结果有助于指导后续语音合成系统的设计与优化。

Abstract: State-of-the-art speech datasets predominantly focus on widely spoken languages, often overlooking low-resource languages such as Luxembourgish, which remain underrepresented in speech technology research. In this work, we introduce LuxEmo, a 21-hour conversational expressive speech corpus for Luxembourgish with 4 emotion categories. LuxEmo is derived from Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL) youth broadcasts, using automated detection followed by human validation. We propose a semi-automatic curation workflow combining voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cues, and targeted human review. Additionally, we benchmark five expressive TTS systems covering German-based cross-lingual transfer, multilingual Luxembourgish support, Luxembourgish adaptation, and non-parametric prosody transfer. Performance is evaluated using both objective metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [50] [DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31980)
*Meng Chen,Anya Ji,Tsung-Han Wu,Tobias Maringgele,David M. Chan,Alane Suhr,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DigitalCoach数据集，用于研究AI模型是否能像人类专家一样教新手使用软件。实验表明，当前模型在教学方式上与人类存在显著差异，且在视觉 groundedness 方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI智能体在自动化软件任务方面能力日益增强，但能否教会人类自己使用软件仍是待探索的问题。现有研究多聚焦于AI替代人类完成任务，而非辅助人类学习技能。该研究旨在填补这一空白，探索AI作为计算机使用教练的可行性。

Method: 构建了DigitalCoach多模态数据集，包含72段人类专家-新手辅导会话，共22,752轮对话，涵盖28.1小时的屏幕和输入事件录制，覆盖5款软件应用。采用自动化评估和交互式评估两种方式，对比模型与人类教练的差异，分析模型在视觉 grounding 和教学互动性上的表现。

Result: 自动化评估显示：模型与人类教练的辅导方式存在差异——模型提供更多直接指令，但更少解释、错误诊断和知识检查问题；当固定辅导方法时，模型产生的语句与人类参考相似，但视觉 groundedness 较差。交互式评估证实：模型教练导致学习者被动跟随指令，缺乏深层参与，且在视觉 grounding 方面明显不足。

Conclusion: DigitalCoach 为研究计算机使用辅导智能体奠定了数据基础。研究表明未来需要开发更具协作性和主动性的辅导智能体，以提升视觉 groundedness 和促进学习者的深层参与。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.

</details>


### [51] [Scalable Behaviour Cloning on Browser Using via Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32014)
*Kaisen Yang,Zheng Jiang,Yuzhao Peng,Houde Qian,Boshi Zhang,Youjie Zheng,Shijin Hong,Qingle Liu,Ruoyu Han,Bohan Lyu,Bingxiang He,Eren Cai,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过技能蒸馏将用户在浏览器上的交互轨迹转化为可复用的自然语言技能，从而构建浏览器智能体的可扩展能力。核心观点是人类交互轨迹中隐含的先验知识可以弥补智能体在不完全信息下决策的不足，技能以图结构组织实现可持续增长而非无限积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器智能体的瓶颈在于不完全信息下的决策能力，而非底层操作能力。用户交互轨迹中已隐含智能体所缺乏的先验知识。

Method: 通过可扩展的行为克隆实现技能蒸馏：将用户交互轨迹转换为紧凑的自然语言技能，并组织成技能图谱结构。

Result: 提出一种通过集体用户技能而非人工设计任务来实现浏览器智能体可扩展性的新范式。

Conclusion: 浏览器智能体的可扩展性可能更多来自于互联网用户已表达的集体技能，而非人工设计的任务。

Abstract: Internet users collectively perform an enormous range of skilled work through web browsers, from software development and document editing to search, forms, and enterprise workflows, making human browsing a highly scalable but under-exploited source of reusable browser skills. We argue that the bottleneck for browser agents is decision-making under incomplete information rather than low-level operation, and that the priors agents lack are already implicit in human interaction traces. We therefore study scalable behavior cloning for browser agents via skill distillation, converting user interaction trajectories into compact natural-language skills that agents can read, retrieve, reuse, and compose directly. We further organize the distilled skills into a skill graph so that growth proceeds through consolidation rather than unbounded accumulation. This suggests that the scalability of browser agents may come less from manually designed tasks and more from the collective skills already expressed by internet users. Our project is available at: https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/.

</details>


### [52] [Generative Skill Composition for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32025)
*Xinyu Zhao,Zhen Tan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillComposer提出结构化技能组合方法，通过约束自回归解码器将技能选择作为序列预测问题，联合解决技能子集、数量和顺序三个维度。在GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview上相比无技能基线分别提升23.1和18.2个百分点，达到与黄金技能检索相当的效果但成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent的技能库日益增长，技能组合选择成为瓶颈。现有方法无法捕捉技能组合的结构化特性——子集选择、数量和顺序三个维度相互耦合且不可分割。

Method: 将结构化技能组合形式化为任务条件下的技能序列预测问题。使用约束自回归解码器遍历技能标识符，使子集、数量和顺序在单次解码中联合生成，自然捕获技能间依赖关系。

Result: 在SkillsBench上，相比无技能基线，GPT-5.2-Codex提升+23.1pp，Gemini-3-Pro-Preview提升+18.2pp。超越top-3检索方法，达到与黄金技能检索相当水平的任务成功率，同时降低了prompt token成本。

Conclusion: 结构化技能组合通过联合预测的方式有效解决了复杂技能选择问题，验证了将技能组合作为序列预测任务的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents benefit from skills for solving complex tasks. Skills encapsulate modular packages of procedural knowledge and instructions for performing specialized tasks, such as setting up a sandboxed environment, running a test suite, or refactoring a function across multiple files. As skill libraries grow and become reusable across tasks and domains, selecting an appropriate skill composition has emerged as a central bottleneck. Existing approaches fall into two categories. One exposes the agent's reasoning to the entire skill collection; the other performs skill retrieval via embeddings or LLM-based rerankers. Both provide useful insights; however, they miss the structural nature of skill composition, which is a joint decision over which skills, how many, and in what order -- three dimensions that cannot be decoupled. We formalize this as structured skill composition: given a task and a skill library, predict an executable skill plan that jointly specifies the activated subset, count, and execution order. We propose SkillComposer, which instantiates structured skill composition as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction. SkillComposer uses a constrained autoregressive decoder over skill identifiers, so subset, count, and order emerge jointly from a single decoding pass, and dependencies between successive skills are captured naturally. We build a training set of task-composition pairs from a real, human-curated skill library. We then evaluate SkillComposer along two axes: composition quality on a held-out test set, and downstream task success on SkillsBench across two production-grade coding agents. On GPT-5.2-Codex, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, SkillComposer raises the pass rate by +23.1, +18.2pp over the no-skill baseline, surpassing top-3 retrieval and matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.

</details>


### [53] [When LLMs Read Tables Carelessly: Measuring and Reducing Data Referencing Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32029)
*Yuqing Yang,Qi Zhu,Zhen Han,Boran Han,Zhengyuan Shen,Shuai Wang,Vassilis N. Ioannidis,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文首次系统评估了大语言模型在表格任务中的数据引用错误(DREs)，发现所有测试模型(1.7B-20B参数)均存在此问题。通过引入数据引用作为评论器的机制，结合批评式过滤和拒绝采样，可将答案准确率提升12.0%。训练了一个轻量级4B评论器模型，在分布内和分布外DRE检测中达到78.2%平均F1分数，有效辅助更大模型的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在表格任务上表现良好，但仍存在数据引用错误(DREs)，即错误引用或遗漏表格数值。这直接损害了推理过程的正确性和可靠性，而此前研究仅提供了有限的小规模分析，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 首次系统评估不同模型和任务中的表格数据引用错误；将数据引用作为评论器(critic)整合到推理流程中；训练了轻量级4B参数评论器模型用于DRE检测。实验覆盖1.7B至20B参数规模的不同模型。

Result: DREs在所有测试模型中均存在；基于评论器的过滤和拒绝采样机制可将答案准确率提升高达12.0%；4B评论器模型在分布内和分布外DRE检测中平均F1分数达78.2%，能有效辅助更大模型的推理。

Conclusion: 数据引用错误是LLM处理表格任务时的普遍问题，轻量级评论器模型可有效检测DREs并提升整体性能，为改进LLM的表格推理能力提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) perform well on table tasks, they still make data referencing errors (DREs), i.e., incorrectly citing or omitting table values, despite understanding the table structure. Beyond final-answer accuracy, DREs directly compromise the correctness and reliability of intermediate reasoning steps. Yet prior studies have only offered limited, small-scale analyses. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of tabular data referencing errors across different models and tasks. Our results show that DREs occur across all tested models (1.7B to 20B parameters). Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating data referencing as a critic significantly improves answer accuracy up to 12.0%, through critic-based filtering and rejection sampling. Finally, we trained a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model that achieves an average F1 score of 78.2% in detecting both in-distribution and out-of-distribution DREs, and effectively assists inference for larger models.

</details>


### [54] [Reinforcement Learning with Metacognitive Feedback Elicits Faithful Uncertainty Expression in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32032)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Avi Caciularu,Gal Yona,Idan Szpektor,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出通过元认知反馈强化学习(RLMF)和元认知数据选择两种新机制来提升LLM的元认知能力,解决模型幻觉、自信过度和不确定性误表达等核心问题。研究将这一方法应用于忠实校准任务,使模型能够准确判断自身性能并相应调整行为。实验表明RLMF在保持准确率的同时,FC任务达到SOTA水平,相比标准RL提升63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在系统性元认知缺陷:高置信度幻觉、无法识别知识边界、误表达内部不确定性。这些问题严重损害了模型的可靠性和可信度。由于监控任务表现并相应调整行为是元认知的核心,研究者认为能够准确评判自身表现的模型更有潜力改进自身性能。

Method: 论文提出两种创新机制:1)元认知反馈强化学习(RLMF),在偏好优化期间根据模型自我评判质量的反馈来优化排序;2)元认知数据选择,利用自我评判识别高价值训练样本。两阶段解耦方法:第一阶段校准模型自我报告置信度的忠实性,第二阶段通过目标输出编辑将置信度映射为自然语言不确定性表达。

Result: 实验表明RLMF在保持准确率的同时,在多样化任务上实现了通用且最先进的FC性能。RLMF相比标准RL提升达63%,同时增强了模型评估和表达自身能力边界的能力。

Conclusion: RLMF是提升LLM元认知的有效范式,可改善模型能力和对齐表现。元认知性能作为RL信号能有效克服先前内在反馈方法的局限。

Abstract: Metacognition is a critical component of intelligence that describes the ability to monitor and regulate one's own cognitive processes. Yet LLMs exhibit systemic deficiencies in key metacognitive faculties: they hallucinate with high confidence, fail to recognize knowledge boundaries, and misrepresent their internal uncertainty--undermining trustworthiness and reliability. Since monitoring task performance and adapting behavior accordingly are central to metacognition, we posit that models capable of accurately judging their own performance are better positioned to improve it. We operationalize this idea via two novel mechanisms: reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF), a paradigm to refine completion rankings during preference optimization based on the quality of a model's self-judgments of performance, and metacognitive data selection, which uses similar self-judgments to identify high-value training examples, outperforming naive active learning. We apply these innovations to the problem of faithful calibration (FC), a task that is itself fundamentally metacognitive: the goal is to align expressed with intrinsic uncertainty, difficult even for frontier LLMs. We adopt a two-stage, decoupled approach, first using these methods to calibrate the faithfulness of models' self-reported confidence scores, then mapping to natural, context-adaptable linguistic uncertainty via targeted output editing. Extensive experiments show RLMF achieves generalizable, state-of-the-art FC on diverse tasks while preserving accuracy. Further, RLMF surpasses standard RL by up to 63% while enhancing models' ability to assess and express their own capability limits. This positions RLMF as a promising paradigm to enhance LLM metacognition toward improved abilities and alignment, and suggests metacognitive performance as an effective RL signal to overcome limits of prior intrinsic feedback methods.

</details>


### [55] [Introspective Coupling: Self-Explanation Training Tracks Behavioral Change Despite Fixed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32038)
*Zifan Carl Guo,Laura Ruis,Jacob Andreas,Belinda Z. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了语言模型（LMs）学习生成预测解释时，何时能产生真正忠实的内省而非表面模仿。通过使用反事实行为作为监督信号，研究发现即使固定训练目标或来自其他模型家族的解释，也能让模型产生忠实于自身当前行为的解释，形成"内省耦合"现象。这种现象在谄媚性和拒绝任务中均出现，且对标签噪声具有鲁棒性，表明反事实解释数据集可为模型内省提供可扩展且可泛化的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成解释时，如何判断其是否真正内省自身行为而非仅仅模仿训练数据？此前研究未充分探索模型能否通过固定的反事实解释数据集学会追踪自身行为变化的能力。

Method: 使用跨输入的反事实行为作为监督信号训练LMs生成特征重要性解释；对比使用自身早期检查点、相似家族模型或固定目标模型产生的反事实解释进行训练；在多个任务（谄媚性、拒绝行为）中测试；添加标签噪声验证鲁棒性。

Result: 使用固定反事实解释训练的模型，其生成解释更忠实于自身当前行为而非训练目标；内省耦合在训练解释与当前行为保持足够相关性时发生；解释能追踪行为变化，无需更新监督信号；该现象在多个任务中对标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 即使固定的反事实解释数据集也能提供可扩展且可泛化的后训练信号，使模型获得内省能力。解释训练与行为训练并行时，解释可自动追踪行为变化，支持将反事实解释作为监控和改进模型行为的实用工具。

Abstract: When does training language models (LMs) to generate explanations of their predictions yield faithful introspection, rather than superficial imitation? We study LMs trained to explain which features of their inputs influenced their behavior, using models' counterfactual behavior on modified inputs as supervision. Surprisingly, we find that LMs trained on fixed counterfactual explanations derived from earlier checkpoints of themselves, or even from behaviorally similar models in different families, frequently produce explanations more faithful to their own current behaviors than to those of their training targets. This "introspective" coupling between LM explanations and behaviors occurs when training explanations remain sufficiently correlated with current behaviors over the course of training, even as behaviors themselves shift. We also show that introspective coupling tracks behavior shifts: when explanation training is provided concurrently with other post-training objectives, explanations track those shifts without requiring updated supervision. This phenomenon appears in multiple tasks, including sycophancy and refusal, and is robust to label noise. Overall, our results show that even fixed datasets of counterfactual explanations can provide scalable and generalizable post-training signal for introspection.

</details>


### [56] [A Single Rewrite Suffices: Empirical Lessons from Production Skill Description Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30775)
*Yangqiaoyu Zhou,Mohammad Alqudah,Kwei-Herng Lai,Aaron Halfaker,Yingqi Xiong,Yaar Harari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个自动优化AI agent技能描述的pipeline，能够以32倍的速度提升匹配人类调优描述的效果，同时识别出需要架构级干预的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI agent需要将用户查询路由到专业技能，通过匹配查询与自然语言技能描述。当两个技能描述重叠时，路由LLM会错误路由查询（称为"技能碰撞"）。随着agent扩展到数十个技能，手动调优描述以维持路由准确性成为重大工程瓶颈。

Method: 在生产环境的企业群聊agent（9个技能，372个回归测试用例）上部署自动描述优化pipeline。对pipeline各组件进行系统性消融实验，同时在ToolBench（16k工具）上验证。通过对比迭代预算、反馈信号组合、混淆对双向编辑、训练集大小等因素的影响，确定关键驱动因素。

Result: Pipeline生成描述平均F1达79.2%，与人工调优描述的79.4% F1相当（平均差异-0.20%，在0.78%多种子噪声范围内）。将每个技能的工程工作量从120分钟降至3.8分钟（32倍加速）。单一LLM重写配合假阳性和假阴性案例即可获得大部分改进，其他设计选择对F1影响均小于0.5%。描述优化无法解决技能意图范围本身重叠的情况。

Conclusion: 自动描述优化pipeline能有效匹配人工调优效果，同时大幅降低工程成本。论文识别出一个诊断指标（训练-验证F1差距大）来标记需要架构级干预而非文本级优化的案例，为实际部署提供了明确的决策依据。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents route user queries to specialized skills by matching queries against natural language skill descriptions. When two skills share overlapping descriptions, the routing LLM misroutes queries, a failure we term skill collision. As agents scale to dozens of skills, manually tuning descriptions to maintain routing accuracy becomes a significant engineering bottleneck. We deploy an automated description optimization pipeline on a production enterprise group chat agent (9 skills, 372 regression cases). The pipeline produces descriptions averaging 79.2% F1, matching manually tuned descriptions at 79.4% F1 (average per-skill difference -0.20%, within the 0.78% multi-seed noise floor), while reducing per-skill engineering effort from 120 minutes to 3.8 minutes (32 times speedup). We then examine which pipeline components actually drive this match. Systematic ablation on both the production system and ToolBench (16k tools) reveals that a single LLM rewrite using any available false-positive and false-negative cases captures most of the available improvement. Other design choices we tested (iteration budget, feedback signal composition, dual editing of confused pairs, and training set size) each affect final F1 by less than 0.5%. Description optimization addresses skill collisions caused by overlapping descriptions but cannot resolve cases where two skills intended scopes genuinely overlap. We identify a diagnostic (a large train-validation F1 gap) that flags the latter cases for architectural rather than text-level intervention.

</details>


### [57] [Indi-RomCoM: Code-Mixed Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Romanized Indic-English Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30790)
*Avisha Das,Mihir Parmar,Mohana Ramnath,Pulkit Verma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Indi-RomCoM 基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在印度语罗马化代码混合（RCM）指令上的表现，揭示了当前LLM在该任务上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在单语和本地文字基准测试上表现优异，但其在处理罗马化代码混合（RCM）内容——即双语使用者用罗马字母混合本地语言与英语进行交流——方面的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了 Indi-RomCoM 基准测试，包含7个指令跟随任务、4种广泛使用的印度语言、3个受控的代码混合强度等级。在零样本和少样本设置下评估了包括专有模型、开源模型和印度语专用模型在内的多种LLM。

Result: LLM在RCM指令上表现持续欠佳，且性能随代码混合密度增加而下降。推理任务的性能下降幅度小于检测任务（如毒性检测），原因是生成的解释提供了必要的上下文信息。

Conclusion: Indi-RomCoM基准测试有助于社区开发更具包容性的多语言系统。

Abstract: Romanized Code Mixing (RCM), where bilingual speakers fluidly blend local languages with English in Roman script, has emerged as the dominant form of communication across multilingual communities. While Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on monolingual and native-script benchmarks, their ability to follow instructions and reason over RCM-based content remains largely unexplored. To this end, we introduce the Indi-RomCoM benchmark for facilitating systematic evaluation on Indic Romanized Code-Mixed instructions. Our benchmark spans seven instruction-following tasks, four widely spoken Indic languages, and three controlled code-mixing intensity levels. We extensively evaluate a suite of LLMs covering proprietary, open-weight, and Indic-focused models under zero- and few-shot settings. LLMs consistently underperform on RCM instructions, with performance degrading as code-mixing density increases. Furthermore, reasoning tasks suffer less degradation than detection tasks (e.g., Toxicity) because the generated explanations offer necessary context. We believe Indi-RomCoM helps the community in developing inclusive multilingual systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [58] [Performance Analysis in Parallel Programming Education: A Comparative Usability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31458)
*Anna-Lena Roth,David James,Jonas Posner,Michael Kuhn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EduMPI，一个旨在简化MPI并行编程教学的学习支持工具。该工具通过直观的图形界面自动化集群程序执行，并提供MPI通信的近实时可视化，帮助学生追踪进程通信并检测性能问题。用户研究表明EduMPI能够降低学习门槛，提升学生对并行程序性能的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高性能计算(HPC)教学中，性能分析工具的复杂性和所需的专业知识（如集群架构、资源管理、MPI和常见并行问题）构成了较高的学习门槛，阻碍了这些工具在教育环境中的整合。

Method: 开发了EduMPI工具，提供直观的GUI界面，自动化集群上的程序执行，并提供MPI通信的近实时可视化，使学生能够根据进程在集群中的物理位置追踪通信并交互式检测性能问题。

Result: 用户研究表明EduMPI降低了学习门槛，培养了学生对并行程序性能的直观理解，增强了教育价值。

Conclusion: EduMPI作为专业性能分析工具的教育替代方案，能够有效辅助MPI并行编程教学，使学生在更易入门的环境中学习并行编程和性能分析。

Abstract: Parallel programming curricula encompass not only the development of parallel code and algorithm design but also emphasize efficiency, optimization, and performance analysis. To equip students with the skills necessary for writing efficient parallel code using message passing with MPI, practical experience on HPC environments is essential. Performance analysis tools assist in identifying issues such as load imbalances or bottlenecks. Despite their use by experienced developers, these tools' complexity and required knowledge of cluster architectures, resource management, MPI, and common parallel issues hinder their educational integration. To address these barriers, we developed EduMPI, a learning support tool designed to simplify cluster usage and performance analysis for students. EduMPI offers an intuitive GUI that automates program execution on clusters and delivers near-real-time visualizations of MPI communication. This enables students to track process communication according to their physical placement within the cluster and detect performance problems interactively. This paper presents a user study comparing EduMPI with established professional performance analysis tools, demonstrating that EduMPI lowers entry barriers and fosters an intuitive understanding of parallel program performance, thereby enhancing its educational value.

</details>


### [59] [LASER: Load-Aware Serving with Early-Exit for Reasoning LLMs at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31580)
*Zhiqing Tang,Size Li,Hanshuai Cui,Zilan Huang,Jianxiong Guo,Tian Wang,Yuan Wu,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LASER通过负载感知的自适应早退出机制和推理预算预分配，在边缘设备部署大推理模型时实现17-38%延迟降低和3-6% SLO提升，精度损失仅1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型部署在边缘设备时，长链式思考(CoT)序列与有限资源之间存在矛盾，现有基于置信度的早退出方法采用固定阈值，仅从单请求角度优化，忽视了边缘服务中多请求并发和负载波动。

Method: 提出LASER框架，包含两个互补设计：(1)负载感知自适应退出阈值，在经验验证的稳健范围内根据实时系统负载调整置信度阈值；(2)难度和负载感知的推理预算预分配，根据请求难度和系统容量分配计算资源。问题被建模为推理质量与服务延迟的联合优化问题。

Result: 在两个推理模型、四个基准测试和多种负载条件下的实验表明，LASER相比固定阈值基线方法，平均延迟降低17-38%，服务级目标(SLO)满意度提升3-6%，而平均精度损失仅约1%。

Conclusion: LASER通过结合自适应阈值调整和推理预算预分配，有效解决了边缘推理服务中资源约束与长CoT序列之间的矛盾，为大推理模型的边缘部署提供了实用的服务策略。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) such as DeepSeek-R1 have achieved strong performance through extended chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. However, deploying them on edge devices raises a conflict between long CoT sequences and constrained resources. Recent confidence-based early exit methods reduce CoT length for individual requests, yet they apply fixed thresholds from a single-request perspective, ignoring multi-request concurrency and load fluctuation in edge serving. To bridge this gap, we propose \underline{L}oad-\underline{A}ware \underline{S}erving with \underline{E}arly-exit for \underline{R}easoning (LASER). LASER couples two complementary designs: (1) a load-aware adaptive exit threshold that adjusts the confidence bar based on real-time system load within an empirically validated robust range, and (2) a difficulty- and load-aware reasoning budget pre-allocation that assigns compute resources by request difficulty and system capacity. We formulate the problem as a joint optimization of reasoning quality and service latency. Experiments on two reasoning models, four benchmarks, and diverse load conditions show that LASER reduces average latency by 17--38\% and improves service-level objective (SLO) satisfaction by 3--6\% over fixed-threshold baselines, at an average accuracy cost of only 1\%.

</details>


### [60] [Decoupling Trust in Byzantine CRDTs: Fine-grained Post-Compromise Handling without Breaking Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31759)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合身份信任与内容信任的细粒度信任模型，用于在拜占庭环境下处理CRDT系统中的后泄露场景，允许副本在保留已接受更新同时实现选择性包含/排除，并保持因果一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CRDT方案假设参与者是良性的，无法应对拜占庭攻击。现有方案在处理后泄露场景时面临困境：追溯性排除先前正确参与者的更新会破坏因果依赖并使后续计算失效。如何在恶意参与者加入后保持系统正确性和数据可用性是核心挑战。

Method: 1) 将身份信任与内容信任解耦；2) 提出细粒度双维度信任模型（身份维度+内容语义维度）；3) 基于确定性重建机制；4) 信任决策可嵌入应用级策略，实现对每个更新影响的精确控制。

Result: 方法支持副本选择性保留或排除更新，既能接纳后泄露前合法参与者的历史贡献，又可过滤恶意更新，同时保持因果一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法在去中心化CRDT系统中实现了对拜占庭行为和故障的鲁棒灵活处理，兼顾了安全性与数据可用性。

Abstract: Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) provide strong eventual consistency without coordination, but classical approaches assume benign participants. In Byzantine settings, convergence is typically enforced through agreement on update validity, often relying on identity-based filtering. However, such approaches struggle in post-compromise scenarios, where a previously correct participant becomes malicious: retroactive exclusion of its updates may break causal dependencies and invalidate subsequent computations. In this paper, we decouple identity-based trust from content-based trust and introduce a fine-grained trust model that combines both dimensions. Building on deterministic reconstruction, our approach allows replicas to preserve previously accepted updates while enabling selective inclusion or exclusion based on both the originating identity (e.g., public keys) and the semantics of individual updates. Trust decisions can incorporate application-level policies, enabling precise control over the impact of each update on the system state. Our approach preserves causal consistency and enables robust and flexible handling of both Byzantine and faulty behavior in decentralized CRDT systems.

</details>

### [61] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控的学生-教师协议，评估自然语言反馈是否真正带来改进，还是仅来自重试或额外计算。研究发现：自我生成的反馈收效甚微，而强大的外部教师才能产生实质性的反馈收益；互动收益更多取决于学生使用反馈的能力，而非教师身份；能否利用反馈是关键瓶颈，而非反馈本身的可得性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮语言智能体设置中，更高的最终准确率可能反映有用的反馈，但也可能仅来自重采样、格式修正或额外的测试时计算。需要区分这些效应，以确定自然语言反馈是否真正产生超越单纯重复尝试的改进。

Method: 跨四个基准测试（Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini、ARC-AGI1）引入受控的学生-教师协议，评估13个开放权重模型，分别担任学生和教师角色。对比外部反馈、自我反馈和无引导自优化，同时变化交互历史、任务难度和教师对特权任务信息的访问权限。构建密集的学生-教师交互矩阵进行分析。

Result: 多轮改进通常不是反馈使用的证据：自我生成的反馈仅略优于无引导自优化；而最强大的外部教师产生显著更大的反馈特异性收益。交互收益更多由学生使用反馈的能力驱动，而非教师身份，但对于固定学生，教师选择仍然重要。

Conclusion: 基于反馈的智能体应以重复尝试基线为对照进行评估；利用反馈的能力（而非仅反馈可得性）是交互改进的核心瓶颈。研究发布了受控的学生-教师评估框架。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [62] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Contrastive Reflection框架，通过对比反思循环优化信息检索Agent的提示词，在HotpotQA上通过单次修复将准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。核心贡献是对比反思循环本身，而非树形切片选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在信息检索中承担检索查询、答案综合和评估判断等任务，但提示词优化在工程实践中更像调试而非盲目搜索。工程师需要了解哪些行为失败、哪些邻近行为仍有效、两者区别以及修改是否在改善测试集性能的同时不引入回归问题，但缺乏系统化的调试框架支持。

Method: Contrastive Reflection是一个迭代式提示优化框架，包含以下核心组件：(1)任务导向的质量定义：QA Agent暴露检索/推理轨迹，Grading Agent暴露维度级评分和理由；(2)基于结构化轨迹识别错误锚定的行为切片，添加同区域的成功样本；(3)让Teacher LLM提出针对性提示修改；(4)仅在验证性能改善时接受候选修改，可选地加入回归检查。框架使用树形切片选择器实例化，但核心贡献是对比反思循环本身。

Result: 在HotpotQA检索增强QA公开数据集上：(1)单次树形选择的对比修复将held-out精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%；(2)仅使用失败案例的变体和改进随机证据的变体改善较少且破坏了更多原本正确的样本；(3)与先进提示优化器相比：MIPROv2达到59.4%，GEPA达到57.0%，方法性能接近这些现代提示优化器。

Conclusion: Contrastive Reflection为IR Agent提供了可检查的、验证驱动的可解释优化循环，使提示词修复更加透明和系统化。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%. Failure-only and random-evidence variants improve less and break more previously correct examples. A light instruction-only comparison places the method near modern prompt optimizers: MIPROv2 reaches 59.4% and GEPA 57.0%. The result is an interpretable optimization loop for IR agents, aimed at making prompt repair more inspectable and validation-driven.

</details>


### [63] [How Can AI Find My Model? A Model-Finding Experimental Study Considering Data Formats, Embeddings, and Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30846)
*Jhon G. Botello,Jose J. Padilla,Erika Frydenlund,Krzysztof Rechowicz,Eric Weisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了基于AI的仿真模型发现问题，评估了数据表示、transformer嵌入模型和检索策略对使用自然语言查询发现仿真模型的影响，为AI驱动的模型可组合性和互操作性提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真模型的重用发现是M&S领域的根本挑战。当大量模型共存时，难以识别与给定建模意图相符的模型。

Method: 实验研究了数据表示、基于transformer的嵌入模型和检索策略对自然语言查询发现仿真模型的影响，使用recall@5和nDCG@5等标准信息检索指标评估性能。

Result: 数据表示很重要；开源嵌入模型可达到高性能；重排序方法很重要，且随查询复杂度增加而愈发重要。

Conclusion: 本研究为AI驱动的模型发现提供了基线，并讨论了其在推进AI驱动的可组合性和互操作性方面的作用。

Abstract: Discovering simulation models for reuse remains a fundamental challenge in Modeling and Simulation (M&S). When many models coexist, identifying those that align with a given modeling intent remains difficult. Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly retrieval-based approaches, offer a promising pathway to operate at this semantic layer. In this paper, we present an experimental study investigating the impact of data representation, transformer-based embedding models, and retrieval strategies on the discovery of simulation models using natural language queries. We evaluated performance across multiple query types using standard information retrieval metrics, including recall@5 and nDCG@5. Results show that data representation matters, open-source embedding models can achieve high performance, and reranking methods are important, especially as query complexity increases. This work provides a baseline for AI-driven model discovery and discusses its role in advancing toward AI-driven composability and interoperability.

</details>


### [64] [BayesBench: Evaluating LLM Belief Trajectories Under Multi-Turn Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30850)
*Ankur Samanta,Akshayaa Magesh,Tal Lancewicki,Ayush Jain,Youliang Yu,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Aditya Modi,Daniel R. Jiang,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入BayesBench基准测试，评估大语言模型在多轮对话中是否像理性贝叶斯推断者一样更新信念。研究发现模型规模提升有助于潜变量推理和证据积累，但推理能力与下游预测能力之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估主要关注单轮对话中的最终答案，忽视了多轮场景下模型如何随新证据更新信念这一关键能力。实际应用中，多轮对话需要模型持续整合信息、推断环境中的未观测量并更新信念。

Method: 研究者构建了BayesBench模拟环境，包含三个递进复杂度的任务：(i)贝叶斯估计——从顺序证据推断未知参数；(ii)贝叶斯预测——将关于潜变量的信念转化为结果预测；(iii)隐框架贝叶斯预测——观察结果经用户人格过滤，需联合推断潜状态和人格。研究测试了7个不同规模(3B-70B)的LLM。

Result: 模型规模扩展提升了潜变量推理和证据积累能力，部分更新可匹配贝叶斯后验分布。然而这些能力无法可靠迁移到下游预测任务，暴露出"推断潜结构"与"理性更新目标结果信念"之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: LLM虽能学习贝叶斯推理的部分能力，但存在推理-预测能力割裂问题。规模扩展虽有帮助，但不足以保证理性信念更新在所有任务上的可靠性。未来需关注如何弥合推断能力与实际决策应用之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically deployed in multi-turn conversations, where each turn provides new evidence that should reduce epistemic uncertainty about their environment. Acting rationally then requires inferring the unobserved quantities that govern it and updating beliefs about them as evidence accumulates. Yet most evaluations only score the model's final-turn answer in a single-turn format, leaving this process unexamined. We ask how closely LLMs' belief updates match those of a rational Bayesian reasoner in multi-turn settings, and introduce BayesBench, a suite of simulation environments that probe this across three progressively complex tasks: (i) Bayesian estimation, where the model infers an unknown parameter from sequential evidence; (ii) Bayesian prediction, where the model turns inferred beliefs about a latent variable into outcome forecasts; and (iii) latent-framed Bayesian prediction, where observations are filtered through a user-persona framing, requiring joint inference over the latent state and the persona. Across seven LLMs (3B--70B), scaling improves latent inference and evidence accumulation, with updates occasionally matching the Bayesian posterior. However, these gains do not reliably carry over to downstream prediction, exposing a gap between inferring latent structure and using it to rationally update beliefs about the target outcome.

</details>


### [65] [When Does Learning to Stop Help? A Cost-Aware Study of Early Exits in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30852)
*Zhe Dong,Fang Qin,Manish Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究推理语言模型中何时学习式停止规则优于简单置信度或收敛阈值，提出LearnStop方法。在18个任务-模型设置中，发现学习式停止对自由形式数学任务有益（GSM8K+Qwen3-32B达+0.157峰值增益），但对多选题和极难任务，标量规则（置信度/熵/稳定性）同样有效或更强。结论是学习式停止的价值取决于轨迹结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在不同实例上消耗不同数量的有效计算，但何时学习式停止规则优于简单置信度或收敛阈值仍不明确。这是部署效率与性能权衡的核心问题。

Method: 提出LearnStop：一种无隐藏状态的检查点停止器。在固定预算检查点处探测当前推理前缀的短答案，并从在线特征预测前缀正确性，包括：答案置信度、熵、前缀投票份额、答案稳定性、回溯标记密度。

Result: 在18个任务-模型设置（GSM8K, MATH-500, MMLU-Pro, AIME-90, GPQA, Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1蒸馏模型）中，结果与任务相关。自由形式数学上，多特征学习停止改善固定预算前沿，GSM8K+Qwen3-32B的后验峰值自适应增益达+0.157；在多选和极难设置上，标量置信度/熵/稳定性规则更有竞争力。

Conclusion: 学习式停止不是标量退出的通用替代品，而是价值取决于轨迹结构的工具。主要实际发现：当许多问题在完整预算前就已正确但不存在单一可靠标量停止信号时，学习式停止有用；当置信度或答案收敛已解决停止问题时，其优势基本消失。

Abstract: Reasoning models spend different amounts of useful computation across instances, but it remains unclear when a learned stopping rule improves over simple confidence or convergence thresholds. We study this question with LearnStop, a hidden-state-free checkpoint stopper for reasoning language models. At fixed budget checkpoints, LearnStop probes a short answer from the current reasoning prefix and predicts prefix correctness from online features such as answer confidence, entropy, prefix vote share, answer stability, and backtracking-marker density. Across 18 task-model settings spanning GSM8K, MATH-500, MMLU-Pro, AIME-90, GPQA, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1 distillations, the answer is task-dependent. On free-form math, learned multi-feature stopping improves the fixed-budget frontier and often beats scalar exits: on GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, the empirical frontier reaches a post-hoc peak adapt gain of +0.157, validation-selected operating points preserve positive gains, and the paired gain over the strongest scalar baseline is +0.028. On multiple-choice and very hard settings, scalar confidence, entropy, or stability rules are competitive or stronger. We therefore frame learned stopping not as a universal replacement for scalar exits, but as a tool whose value depends on trajectory structure. We further provide validation-selected operating points, paired bootstrap tests, finite-grid lost-correct risk calibration, cost accounting under KV-fork, prefix-cache, and black-box regimes, H100 serving profiles, checkpoint-schedule sweeps, transfer analyses, and robustness checks. The main practical finding is that learned stopping is useful when many questions become correct before full budget but do not exhibit a single reliable scalar stopping signal; its benefits largely disappear when confidence or answer convergence already solves the stopping problem.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond expert users: agents should help users construct preferences, not just elicit them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30863)
*Irena Saracay,Ludwig Schmidt,Carlos Guestrin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文挑战了智能助手假设用户具有完整偏好的传统观点，提出用户往往缺乏领域知识来明确表达偏好，需要通过对话交互学习后才能形成偏好。作者提出CoPref模型和CoShop基准测试，结果显示即使是最先进的五个模型在五轮交互后准确率也不超过56%，主要失败原因不是找不到物品，而是未能帮助用户扩展对自身需求的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统agent系统假设用户是专家，具有完整且明确的偏好，当任务描述不完整时会直接询问用户澄清问题。但这一假设不现实——用户在许多领域缺乏必要的知识来形成完整的偏好，因此无法回答agent的澄清问题。需要重新审视这种人机交互范式。

Method: 1. 借鉴信息经济学中的"搜索-体验-信任"(Search-Experience-Credence)框架，构建CoPref模型，形式化描述用户如何基于agent的对话行为来构建偏好。
2. 在推荐系统场景中设计CoShop交互基准测试，模拟用户与agent的对话推荐过程，评估agent帮助用户获取领域知识的能力。

Result: 测试了5个前沿模型，结果显示：
1. 即使经过5轮交互，所有模型的准确率都不超过56%
2. 失败原因主要不是agent找不到合适的物品
3. 关键问题在于agent未能有效扩展用户对自身需求的认知

Conclusion: 当前agent系统的核心缺陷不在于信息检索能力，而在于缺乏帮助用户学习领域知识、形成完整偏好的交互策略。需要设计新的机制让agent主动通过示例、解释等方式帮助用户获取决策所需的领域知识，从而能够更好地完成推荐和任务规划。

Abstract: Agents typically assume an expert user -- one with well-formed preferences about what they want -- and default to clarifying questions whenever the task is underspecified. We argue this assumption is unrealistic. Users often lack the domain knowledge to have completely specified preferences; if asked about their preference on some feature, the user may be unable to answer without the agent helping the user to learn some domain knowledge needed to form a preference for that feature, e.g., via examples or explanations. To formalize these principles, we draw on the Search-Experience-Credence framework from Information Economics to introduce CoPref, a model of how users construct preferences based on agent dialog actions. We then study these ideas concretely in agentic recommender systems, proposing CoShop, an interactive benchmark. In CoShop, an agent converses with and makes recommendations for a CoPref user. The agent's performance depends on whether it can help the user gain the knowledge needed to specify the task well. Evaluating five frontier models, we find that no agent exceeds 56% accuracy on CoShop despite five turns of interaction. Failures stem not from agents' ability to find items, but from how little the interaction expands what users know about what they want.

</details>


### [67] [Investigating Multi-Agent Deliberation in Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30906)
*Cor Steging,Ludi van Leeuwen,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于LLM的多智能体 deliberation 方法在法律推理任务中的应用,提出了两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的多智能体框架。实验表明,多智能体框架能产生与基线模型相当但显著不同的答案,两者各有擅长的案例类型,多智能体方法尤其适合需要多角度批判性思考的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在法律领域的应用日益广泛,但多智能体方法在该领域仍属探索阶段。现有法律AI研究多聚焦于单一模型,而法律论证本质上涉及多方参与和多方视角的互动,这为多智能体架构提供了天然的应用场景。

Method: 研究者探索了Multi-Agent Deliberation (MAD)方法,并设计了两个新颖的多智能体框架,灵感分别来源于:1)法庭程序(courtroom procedures),模拟法官、律师等多方角色互动;2)法律论证框架,模拟法律论证中的多元观点交锋。实验在法律和非法律基准数据集上进行评估。

Result: 多智能体框架在整体性能上与大型语言模型基线相当,但产生了显著不同的答案。两种方法存在互补性:多智能体能成功解决基线失败的案例,反之亦然。定性分析表明,多智能体方法在需要多角度批判性思考的问题上表现更优。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统是法律领域AI应用的有前景方向,法律启发的多智能体 deliberation 方法展现出独特价值,为AI辅助司法实践和提升司法公正提供了新思路。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence is increasingly applied to the field of law, and has the potential to increase access to justice. One particular movement that is gaining traction is that of agentic AI, wherein AI agents, based on Large Language Models (LLMs) can take autonomous actions. In particular, multi-agent approaches in the legal domain remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate multi-agent deliberation methods for legal reasoning tasks using LLMs. We explore multi-agent deliberation (MAD) and introduce two novel multi-agent frameworks inspired by courtroom procedures and legal argumentation. Our experiments on both legal and non-legal benchmarks reveal that multi-agent frameworks achieve comparable overall performance to baseline large language models, but produce significantly distinct answers. Notably, these approaches can successfully solve cases that the baseline fails to address, and vice versa. We conduct a qualitative evaluation and highlight scenarios where multi-agent frameworks outperform monolithic approaches. For example, multi-agent approaches appear better suited for answering questions that require critical thinking from multiple perspectives. Our work positions multi-agent systems as a promising direction for AI in the legal domain, while demonstrating the potential of law-inspired multi-agent approaches for deliberation.

</details>


### [68] [Why Solve It Twice? Hierarchical Accumulation of Skills for Transfer-Efficient ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30911)
*Yongbin Kim,Yashar Talebirad,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE是一个分层多智能体系统,通过三层知识组织(全局、领域、竞赛特定)解决ML竞赛中每次都需要冷启动的问题。实验证明,分层加载比扁平加载性能提升显著,且知识积累可减少22%的优化迭代,随着技能库增长Agent采纳率从42%提升至85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML工程智能体在每次竞赛中都面临冷启动问题,导致计算资源浪费在重新发现已知技术上,无法有效复用跨竞赛积累的知识。

Method: HASTE提出三层知识架构:全局层、领域层和竞赛特定层,各层耦合对应级别的Agent,由编排器协调领域专家,并通过LLM驱动抽象实现层间知识迁移。系统在冷启动时无积累技能,通过warm-start从先前竞赛中重载技能进行迁移学习。

Result: 控制实验显示,固定159项技能清单,分层加载在8个竞赛中达到100%获奖率,而扁平加载仅62.5%(与无技能加载相当),且消耗2倍输出token。在22个Kaggle竞赛的MLE-Bench Lite基准上,HASTE使用Claude Sonnet 4.6在每竞赛12小时限制下达到77.3%获奖率。Warm-start减少52%优化迭代,Agent采纳率从低技能库存的42%提升至50+技能时的85%。

Conclusion: 更好的知识组织可以部分替代模型强度和计算预算,这表明分层知识管理对智能体能力的重要性。

Abstract: ML engineering agents waste compute rediscovering known techniques because every competition is a cold start. We present HASTE, a hierarchical multi-agent system that organizes cross-competition knowledge into three scope tiers (global, domain, and competition-specific), each coupled to a matching agent level. An orchestrator coordinates domain specialists and promotes learning between tiers via LLM-driven abstraction. A controlled ablation provides evidence for scoped loading: holding a 159-skill inventory constant across 8 competitions, tiered loading achieves a 100% medal rate while flat loading reaches only 62.5%, the same medal rate as loading no skills, and consumes 2x the output tokens. On the full MLE-Bench Lite benchmark (22 Kaggle competitions), HASTE reaches a medal rate of 77.3% using Claude Sonnet 4.6 at 12h per competition. In a cold-start run, the system begins with no accumulated skills. In warm-start runs, it reloads skills learned from earlier competitions, using only global and domain-level skills for transfer across competitions. Warm starts use 52% fewer refinement iterations, and the fraction of proposed changes kept by the agent rises from 42% at low inventory to 85% once 50+ skills are available. These results suggest that better knowledge organization can partly substitute for model strength and compute budget in ML-engineering agents.

</details>


### [69] [RoPoLL: Robust Panel of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30931)
*Anish Acharya,Kris W Pan,Brian Verkhovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RoPoLL方法，通过将LLM评审员小组的聚合函数从共识分数替换为几何中位数等稳健均值估计器，解决了LLM评审系统在污染模型下的有界偏差问题。在13个模型、3个基准、4种腐败机制下的实验证明RoPoLL显著优于传统PoLL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Jury (PoLL) 作为LLM评估的实用替代方案，其统计行为理解不足。当单个评审员出现LLM典型失败模式（模式崩溃、谄媚、安全拒绝）时，PoLL会产生无界偏差，这是一个关键的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 在Huber污染模型下形式化LLM Jury问题，采用经典稳健均值估计框架：将PoLL的小组保留但替换聚合函数为几何中位数(GM)，这是一个无需调参、具有最优有限样本崩溃点1/2的稳健估计器。

Result: 推导出有限样本误差界和匹配的信息论极小极大下界，都以sigma*sqrt(d/N)的参数速率收敛；在崩溃点下界上存在sqrt(d)的统计-计算差距。实验显示：(1)13个开源评审员(4B-675B)中，RoPoLL在所有偏差腐败类型上均优于PoLL；(2)交叉维度攻击下提升约19%；(3)重尾拜占庭对手下提升数个数量级；(4)3-judge RoPoLL (38B参数) 在30%双峰随机腐败下以1.31x的优势超越Mistral-Large-3 (675B)，以18x参数劣势获得更好精度。

Conclusion: RoPoLL通过简单的聚合函数替换即可有效应对LLM评审中的偏差污染，无需额外的人类标注(Noisy-GT控制验证)，为构建可靠的LLM评估系统提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: The LLM Jury, a Panel of LLM Evaluators (PoLL) reporting consensus scores, has become a practical alternative to single-judge LLM evaluation, yet its statistical behavior remains poorly understood. We formalize the LLM Jury under the Huber contamination model and show that PoLL incurs unbounded bias
  under any positive contamination, regardless of jury size, whenever a single judge fails in a biased, LLM-typical way (mode collapse, sycophancy, safety refusal). Framing jury consensus as classical robust mean estimation, we propose RoPoLL (Robust Panel of LLM-as-Judge), which preserves the PoLL
  panel but replaces the aggregation function with a robust mean estimator, instantiated with the geometric median (GM): tuning-free, with the optimal finite-sample breakdown point 1/2. A finite-sample error bound and a matching information-theoretic minimax lower bound agree on the parametric rate
  sigma*sqrt(d/N) and differ on the breakdown floor by a factor of sqrt(d), a statistical-computational gap that polynomial-time RoPoLL pays relative to the intractable Tukey halfspace median. Across 13 open-weight judges (4B-675B), three reward-model benchmarks, and four corruption regimes at rates up
  to 50%, RoPoLL dominates PoLL on every biased corruption type: by about 19% on cross-dimensional attacks at matched compute, and by orders of magnitude on heavy-tailed Byzantine adversaries. A 3-judge RoPoLL committee at 38B beats Mistral-Large-3 (675B) by 1.31x on HelpSteer-2 under 30% bimodal-random
  corruption, an 18x parameter advantage at better accuracy; a Noisy-GT control confirms the premium is paid against biased contamination, not benign imprecision.

</details>


### [70] [AgRefactor: Self-Evolving Agentic Workflow for HLS Compatibility and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30949)
*Yang Zou,Zijian Ding,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgRefactor是一个基于LLM的多智能体工作流，用于将软件代码重构为HLS（高层次综合）兼容程序。它通过自演化记忆系统和集成自动化重构工具来提高效率和可扩展性，在11个基准测试中的9个上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将真实世界的软件转换为可综合的HLS代码仍然具有挑战性，原因包括语言支持受限以及软件和硬件编程实践之间的差距。现有的自动化和基于LLM的重构方法缺乏灵活性、难以扩展且计算成本高。

Method: AgRefactor采用多智能体工作流，结合自演化记忆系统（积累和检索跨任务的事实性和策略性知识）以及自动化重构工具，使智能体能够在LLM驱动的重写和高效的基于工具的转换之间取得平衡。

Result: 在11个具有挑战性的真实世界基准测试中的9个上，AgRefactor优于或匹配最先进的自动化重构工具和基于相同框架骨干构建的强LLM基线。进一步优化后，相比SoTA pragma调优工具实现6.51x的几何平均加速，相比优化的开源设计实现1.20x加速，同时资源增加不到20%。

Conclusion: AgRefactor是全自动的且已开源，为软件到HLS的转换提供了一种更高效、更灵活、更具成本效益的解决方案。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) provides a fast path from concepts to silicon, but converting real-world software into synthesizable HLS code remains challenging due to restrictive language support and the gap between software and hardware programming practices. Existing automated and LLM-based refactoring approaches partially address this problem, yet they often lack flexibility, struggle to scale, and incur high computational costs. We introduce AgRefactor, an LLM-based multi-agent workflow for refactoring software into HLS-compatible programs. AgRefactor incorporates a self-evolving memory system that accumulates and retrieves factual and strategic knowledge across tasks, improving robustness and efficiency on unseen programs. To reduce cost and enhance scalability, it integrates automated refactoring tools, enabling agents to balance LLM-driven rewrites with efficient tool-based transformations. On 9 out of 11 challenging real-world benchmarks, which are 5-10x longer than the most complex cases studied in prior work, AgRefactor outperforms or matches the state-of-the-art automated refactoring tool and a strong LLM-based baseline built on the same framework backbone. Further agentic performance optimization yields a 6.51x geometric mean speedup over the SoTA pragma tuning tool and a 1.20x speedup over optimized open-source designs with less than 20% extra resources. AgRefactor is fully-automated and open-sourced.

</details>


### [71] [Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network: Explainable Deep Learning for Cybersecurity Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30953)
*Nicolaie Popescu-Bodorin,Madeleine Togher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NBS-RASN是一种混合神经架构，通过12层80个神经元实现可解释的网络安全风险评估，通过5个认识论公理作为硬约束保证可解释性，在OWASP Top 10:2025数据集上达到0.79-0.97置信度，证明浅层网络配合深度推理可超越不透明深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型在网络安全领域以牺牲可解释性换取准确性，但高风险领域需要可解释的AI系统，现有的后置解释方法不足

Method: 构建12层80个神经元的浅层网络，编码领域知识、因果推理和专家判断；使用门控机制强制执行5个认识论公理（精确性、因果性、可证伪性、透明性、完整性）作为硬约束；引入残差注意力机制实现深度学习特征；输出完全可分解的风险评分，包含确定性权重分量和可追溯到特定放大器（blast radius、传播速度、结构性质等6个维度）的专家调整

Result: 在覆盖OWASP Top 10:2025所有类别和语言风险类的20个开源项目上验证，达到0.79-0.97的置信分数，可解释性由设计保证而非训练算法

Conclusion: 浅层网络配合深度推理可超越不透明深度模型，挑战了深度学习需要深层网络的假设，证明了在解释性至关重要的高风险网络安全场景中，可解释性与高性能可以兼得

Abstract: We introduce the Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network (NBS-RASN), a hybrid neural architecture for explainable cybersecurity risk assessment in open-source ecosystems. Unlike deep models that trade interpretability for accuracy, our shallow network encodes domain knowledge, causal reasoning, and expert judgment as differentiable components. It uses 80 interpretable neurons across 12 layers, including a gatekeeper that enforces five epistemological axioms - precision, causality, falsifiability, transparency, and completeness - as hard constraints before propagation. Despite limited depth, the network exhibits deep-learning traits via residual attention and feedback loops, learning complex risk patterns without becoming a black box. It produces fully decomposable scores: a deterministic weighted component plus an expert adjustment, with each adjustment traceable to named amplifiers (blast radius, propagation speed, structural nature, default exposure, exploitation pattern, institutional criticality). We validate on 20 open-source projects covering all OWASP Top 10:2025 categories and language risk classes, achieving confidence scores of 0.79-0.97, and show that explainability is guaranteed by design, not by a training algorithm. This challenges the assumption that deep learning requires deep networks, proving that shallow networks with deep reasoning can outperform opaque models in high-stakes cybersecurity, where interpretability is essential.

</details>


### [72] [HyPOLE: Hyperproperty-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30966)
*Arshia Rafieioskouei,Tzu-Han Hsu,Matthew Lucas,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyPOLE框架，利用超属性（hyperproperty）和HyperLTL时序逻辑的形式化规范来指导多智能体强化学习（MARL）在部分可观测环境下的学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MARL方法依赖奖励塑形（reward shaping）来引导学习，存在缺乏数学严谨性、表达能力有限、无法定义达成目标的策略等问题。形式化规范虽有上述优势，但在MARL领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出HyPOLE框架，将超属性（如HyperLTL时序逻辑）的表达力与集中式训练分散式执行（CTDE）技术相结合，合成去中心化策略。

Result: 在SMAC、MessySMAC和WildFire基准测试上的评估表明，HyPOLE相比基线方法具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 该工作将形式化验证领域的超属性规范成功引入MARL，为多智能体学习提供了更具表达力和严谨性的学习框架。

Abstract: Formal specification is a powerful tool to guide the learning process and provides significant advantages over reward shaping: (1) mathematical rigor; (2) expressiveness to specify objectives and constraints, and (3) the ability to define tactics to achieve objectives. However, these benefits remain largely unexplored in the context of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). This paper introduces HyPOLE, a novel framework for MARL under partial observability, where learning is guided by the expressive power of the so-called hyperproperties and, in particular, the temporal logic HyperLTL. We integrate Centralized Training for Decentralized Execution (CTDE) techniques with HyPOLE to synthesize decentralized policies, and our evaluation on SMAC, MessySMAC, and WildFire benchmark demonstrates clear advantages over baselines.

</details>


### [73] [AgentBound: Verifiable Behavioral Governance for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30970)
*Anuj Kaul,Qianlong Lan,Pranay Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBound是一个运行时治理框架，为自主AI智能体提供可验证的行为监督机制，通过三个独立权威机构（委托授权、所有者签署的行为宪章和站点行动合约）的保守组合决策，判断操作是否允许执行，并生成加密可验证的治理凭证以实现问责制和独立重放验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体越来越多地为人类委托方执行金融交易、外部通信和企业工作流等重大操作。现有的智能体基础设施依赖身份联合和委托授权来认证工作负载和控制资源访问，但无法判断在当前行为和操作上下文下已授权的操作是否应该被执行。

Method: AgentBound通过三个独立权威机构的组合进行决策：(1)委托授权 (2)所有者签署的行为宪章 (3)站点行动合约。这些判断通过形式化决策模型保守组合，确定操作是在执行前被允许、需审查还是被拒绝。框架引入持续委托机制，允许长时间运行的智能体在保留撤销权和有限授权的同时，按照持续刷新的治理策略运行周期性工作负载。

Result: 提出了形式化基础、系统架构、治理凭证协议，以及AgentBound-Bench基准测试框架，用于评估治理正确性、权威组合和问责制。

Conclusion: AgentBound并非替代模型对齐，而是通过在授权与执行之间提供确定性治理层来补充模型对齐，将治理从必须被信任的过程转变为可被独立验证的过程。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly perform consequential actions on behalf of human principals, including financial transactions, external communications, and enterprise workflows. Existing agent infrastructure relies on identity federation and delegated authorization to authenticate workloads and control resource access, but it cannot determine whether an authorized action should be executed under the current behavioral and operational context.
  We present AgentBound, a runtime governance framework that provides verifiable behavioral oversight for autonomous AI agents. AgentBound evaluates each proposed action using three independent authorities: delegated authorization, owner-signed behavioral constitutions, and site action contracts. Their judgments are conservatively composed through a formal decision model to determine whether an action should be permitted, reviewed, or denied before execution.
  To provide accountability, AgentBound generates cryptographically verifiable governance receipts that bind every action to the exact delegation, policy, and semantic artifacts governing the decision, enabling independent replay verification and policy provenance. The framework also introduces standing delegation for long-running agents, allowing periodic workloads to operate under continuously refreshed governance policies while preserving revocability and bounded authority.
  We present the formal foundation, system architecture, governance receipt protocol, and AgentBound-Bench, a benchmark framework for evaluating governance correctness, authority composition, and accountability. Rather than replacing model alignment, AgentBound complements it by providing a deterministic governance layer between authorization and execution, transforming governance from a process that must be trusted into one that can be independently verified.

</details>


### [74] [When Regulation Has Memory: Hysteresis and Control Burden in Artificial Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30975)
*Veronique Ziegler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，自适应智能体的调节负担存在路径依赖性：相同目标状态可能需要不同的控制努力，具体取决于智能体是走向还是返回该状态。预防性调节（扰动前稳定化）比反应性调节（扰动后恢复）需要更少的自适应增益。评估自适应智能体应同时考虑其稳定性及其维持稳定所需的调节成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应智能体通常仅根据其行为结果来评判，但该研究指出两个达到相似内部状态的系统可能需要截然不同的纠正性控制来维持该状态。这种隐藏的调节负担对于在噪声、延迟或需求变化环境下运行的智能体至关重要。

Method: 采用自适应不确定性调节的计算模型，将人工智能体驱动通过不确定性目标的连续变化，然后在不重置智能体的情况下逆转变化。通过这一设计创建了简单的

Result: 模拟结果显示明显的路径依赖效应：调节智能体所需的自适应增益形成可重复的滞后环，意味着相同目标在智能体向更具挑战性 regime 移动或从中返回时需要不同的控制水平。调节时机也很关键：在扰动暴露前进行稳定化所需的调节需求显著低于仅在扰动发生后恢复的情况。自适应增益的路径依赖性更为显著，而预防性调节的核心优势在于以更低的控制需求实现可比的调节行为。

Conclusion: 自适应智能体的评估应不仅关注其是否保持有序状态，还应考虑维持有序状态所需的调节投入。研究为理解调节成本的时间依赖性提供了计算模型基础。

Abstract: Adaptive agents are usually judged by what they do, but an agent can appear stable while the internal effort required to keep it stable is increasing. This hidden regulatory burden matters for artificial agents operating under noise, delay, or changing demands: two systems may reach similar internal states while one requires much more corrective control to get there.
  Here, we study whether that burden depends on history. Using a computational model of adaptive uncertainty regulation, we drive an artificial agent through a continuous change in its uncertainty target and then reverse the change without resetting the agent. This creates a simple test for carryover: does the controller respond only to the current target, or does the path by which the agent reached that target still matter?
  The simulations show a clear history-dependent effect. The adaptive gain required to regulate the agent forms a reproducible hysteresis loop, meaning that the same target can require different levels of control depending on whether the agent is moving toward or returning from a more demanding regime. The timing of regulation also matters.
